*Chen Wang,Zhaochun Li,Jionghao Bai,Yining Zhang,Hexuan Deng,Ge Lan,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为蒸馏强化学习（Distilled RL）的新方法，通过将教师监督整合到强化学习目标中，实现了细粒度的知识转移，克服了传统强化学习在知识获取方面的局限性以及在线策略蒸馏在跨模型蒸馏时的困难。实验证明，该方法在多种设置下均显著优于现有的强化学习和在线策略蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后训练方法主要分为强化学习和在线策略蒸馏两种范式。强化学习依赖粗粒度的结果监督，导致信用分配困难且获取新知识能力有限；在线策略蒸馏无条件地通过KL散度匹配教师logits，导致相似教师提供的新知识少，而差异较大的教师往往指导无效，限制了在线策略蒸馏主要局限于同族模型之间的蒸馏。

Method: 提出了蒸馏强化学习（Distilled RL），将教师监督整合到RL目标中，提供细粒度指导，避免无条件模仿。包含三个关键组件：1）带裁剪的反向重要性采样；2）负样本重置；3）序列级几何归一化。

Result: 通过简洁可解释的案例研究证明，蒸馏强化学习能有效将教师模型中原本不可获得的知识转移给学生模型。在包括同族和跨族蒸馏的广泛实验中，蒸馏强化学习在pass@1和pass@k指标上均显著优于标准强化学习和在线策略蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: 蒸馏强化学习成功解决了传统强化学习在知识获取方面的局限和在线策略蒸馏在跨模型蒸馏时的困难，实现了更有效的知识迁移，为LLM后训练提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) post-training is essential for improving reasoning, adaptation, and alignment. Existing methods mainly follow two paradigms: reinforcement learning (RL) and on-policy distillation (OPD). However, RL relies on coarse-grained outcome supervision, resulting in difficult credit assignment and limited capability to acquire new knowledge. OPD, meanwhile, unconditionally matches teacher logits through KL divergence, which creates a dilemma: similar teachers provide little new knowledge, while substantially different teachers often yield ineffective guidance, largely restricting OPD to within-family distillation. We propose Distilled Reinforcement Learning (Distilled RL), which integrates teacher supervision into the RL objective to provide fine-grained guidance, selectively transfer new knowledge and avoid unconditional imitation. Distilled RL contains three components: reverse importance sampling with clipping, negative sample reset, and sequence-level geometric normalization. Through a concise and interpretable case study, we demonstrate that Distilled RL can effectively transfer previously unavailable knowledge from a teacher model to a student model. Extensive experiments across both within-family and cross-family distillation settings show that Distilled RL substantially outperforms standard RL and OPD in terms of both pass@1 and pass@k. Our code is available at https://github.com/597358816/Distilled-RL.

</details>


### [124] [AIGB-R1: Self-Evolving Generative Auto-Bidding via Hierarchical Planner-Executor Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17281)
*Yuejia Dou,Hesong Wang,Xinyu Zhang,Tianyu Wang,Zhilin Zhang,Chuan Yu,Jian Xu,Bo Zheng,Qi Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AIGB-R1是一种分层自演化自动出价框架，利用LLM的推理能力提升AI生成出价策略，通过规划器模块和执行器模块实现从宏观策略规划到细粒度决策的优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成出价（AIGB）范式受限于离线数据集的模式覆盖不足和任务状态理解不充分，而直接应用LLM面临数值精度有限、幻觉和推理延迟等挑战。

Method: 提出AIGB-R1分层自演化框架，包含高层规划器和低层执行器；设计基于经验的自我演化循环；采用离线预训练和在线对齐的两阶段流程；构建交互式竞价模拟环境；提出解耦组相对策略优化（D-GRPO）实现端到端优化。

Result: 在大型公开数据集上的实验结果验证了AIGB-R1的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架成功利用LLM推理能力克服了传统AIGB的局限性，通过分层规划和自演化机制实现了更优的自动出价策略。

Abstract: Auto-bidding plays an essential role in online advertising, automatically adjusting bids for advertisers to optimize their commercial goals. The emerging AI-Generated Bidding (AIGB) paradigm widely adopts generative modeling to optimize bidding strategies, yet suffers from the limited mode coverage of offline datasets and inadequate task-state understanding, hindering effective exploration of optimal strategies. Large Language Models (LLMs), with prior world knowledge and reasoning capabilities, offer a promising approach to overcome these limitations. However, directly applying LLMs to auto-bidding tasks faces inherent challenges in limited numerical precision, hallucinations, and inference latency. To address these limitations, we propose AIGB-R1, a hierarchical self-evolving auto-bidding framework aiming to enhance AI-Generated Bidding via LLMs' Reasoning capabilities, comprising a high-level Planner module for macro-level strategy planning and a low-level Executor module for fine-grained decision-making. Building upon this, we design an experience-driven self-evolving loop, enabling autonomous strategy exploration and optimization from accumulated experience. We adopt a two-stage pipeline of offline pre-training and post-training alignment, and build an interactive bidding simulation environment for strategy rollout. Furthermore, we propose Decoupled Group Relative Policy Optimization (D-GRPO) to achieve end-to-end optimization via advantage decoupling. Experimental results on a large-scale public dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of AIGB-R1.

</details>


### [125] [Rationalizing Boltzmann Rationality: An Axiomatic Characterization of Entropy-Regularized Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17316)
*Silviu Pitis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从经济和信息理论视角，通过区分环境随机性（机会）与决策随机性（选择），将独立性公理限制到环境彩票上，证明了在独立无关性（IIA）和单调性条件下，策略和价值函数唯一确定 Boltzmann 策略、熵正则化表示和软贝尔曼方程。软硬贝尔曼方程的选择本质上归结为设计决策：是否赋予代理选择能力以价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: softmax 策略在强化学习中广泛使用，但缺乏从第一性原理出发的理论推导。熵正则化软贝尔曼方程违反了支持 MDP 奖励结构的独立性公理，存在基本张力。作者试图从经济决策理论和信息论角度解决这一矛盾。

Method: 区分两种随机性：环境随机性（机会）和决策随机性（选择）。将冯·诺依曼-摩根斯坦独立性限制到环境彩票上，施加独立无关性（IIA）和单调性到策略和价值函数，证明这唯一确定 Boltzmann 策略、熵正则化表示和软贝尔曼方程。

Result: 在 IIA 和单调性条件下，策略和价值函数唯一确定 Boltzmann 策略；软贝尔曼方程的选择归结为设计决策：是否赋予代理选择能力以价值。获得 RL 特定后果：回报单调性和广义折扣下的收敛性。

Conclusion: 软硬贝尔曼方程的选择本质上是一个设计决策而非理论冲突。该工作将经济学和信息理论中独立发展的结构统一，提供了 IIA 在智能体设计中适用性的规范性评估。

Abstract: The softmax policy $π(a \mid s) \propto \exp(βQ(s,a))$ is the default model of stochastic choice in reinforcement learning (RL). Various justifications based on robustness, exploration, and optimization have been offered in the RL literature, but none uniquely derives the softmax form from first principles. This leaves a basic tension unresolved: the entropy bonus in the soft Bellman equation violates the Independence axiom that underwrites the Markov decision process (MDP) reward structure. We dissolve this tension by distinguishing two kinds of randomness: chance and choice. By restricting von Neumann-Morgenstern (VNM) Independence to environmental lotteries over base prospects, we show that imposing independence of irrelevant alternatives (IIA) and monotonicity on the policy and value functions at choice nodes uniquely determines the Boltzmann policy, the entropy-regularized representation, and the soft Bellman equation. The choice between the soft and hard Bellman equations thus reduces to a design decision: whether the agent values its own ability to choose. We develop RL-specific consequences, including return monotonicity and convergence under generalized discounting, and synthesize the independent lines from economics and information theory that arrive at the same structure, offering a normative assessment of when IIA is appropriate for agent design.

</details>


### [126] [Grounded verification of chemical and materials reasoning: detection is the bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17417)
*Can Polat,Mustafa Kurban,Erchin Serpedin,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 大语言模型在化学推理中会产生分子式等对象的幻觉，特别是在长尾实体上。本文提出了一种分层验证器，通过从权威数据库和物理规则检查每个可验证主张，并使用门控修正循环来减少错误，将分子式错误从22%降低到4%，检索次数减少3.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在化学推理中会产生幻觉（confabulate），特别是在长尾实体上，这些地方模型的置信度最低。现有的解决方案通常需要全量检索，成本较高；而本文提出的方法可以在更少检索次数下修复错误。

Method: 提出分层验证器：1) 提取每个可验证的主张；2) 与权威数据库和物理规则检查；3) 将参考信息输入门控修正循环。验证器范围可配置，可检查从中间步骤到最终交付物的所有内容。

Result: 在4个模型和528个条件固定提示上，门控修正将已提交的分子式错误从22%降低到4%，检索次数减少3.2倍，击败了对话式预言机。修复在标记触发时成功（80-97%），瓶颈在于检测召回率。Grounding提升最终答案准确率（83%到90%），在同位素半衰期等长尾问题上提升显著（11%到0%）。

Conclusion: 修复的成功率很高（80-97%），瓶颈不在修复而在检测召回率。只有当验证器的范围覆盖到最终交付物时，grounding才能提高最终答案准确率。该方法在长尾实体上效果显著，但在物理常数等已接近上限的领域提升有限。

Abstract: Large language models confabulate chemical objects (molecular formulas, space groups, formation energies) in fluent reasoning traces, concentrated on long-tail entities where confidence is least trustworthy. Deterministic, database-grounded verification can catch and repair such errors without the coverage cost of blanket retrieval; the binding constraint, we find, is detection, not repair. Our tiered verifier extracts each checkable claim, checks it against authoritative databases and physics, and feeds the reference into a gated correction loop. Across four models and 528 condition-pinned prompts, gated correction cuts committed-formula error from 22% to 4% at $3.2\times$ fewer retrievals than blanket augmentation, beating a conversational oracle. Repair succeeds wherever a flag fires (80--97%); the bottleneck is in-loop detection recall. Grounding improves the final answer only when the verifier's scope reaches the deliverable (83% to 90%), and the lift appears only where extractable long-tail error exists: absent on near-ceiling physical constants, large on isotope half-lives (11% to 0%).

</details>


### [127] [Kernelized Linear Attention: Breaking the Capacity Wall with Symmetric Cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17419)
*Ayoub Ghriss,Sourav Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KATA通过将注意力召回建模为球堆积问题并利用自对偶锥认证非负注意力权重，在保持常数时间递归推理的同时实现了高效的关联记忆能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力模型虽然能实现常数时间的递归推理，但在关联记忆任务上性能急剧下降。作者希望解决这一能力与干扰之间的权衡问题。

Method: 1) 将注意力召回建模为球堆积问题；2) 引入KATA框架，利用自对偶齐次锥从第一性原理推导特征图；3) 证明秩一正半定特征具有理想的能力-干扰权衡；4) 恢复无参数凸输出门；5) 通过Welch干扰下限表征关联能力；6) 使用融合Triton内核实现两种操作模式（FlashAttention风格和精确O(T)分块状态形式）。

Result: 1) KATA恢复无参数凸输出门并支持指数级密钥的球码；2) FlashAttention风格前向计算速度可达FlashAttention-2的~1.6倍；3) 精确O(T)分块状态形式在131k令牌时达到~11× FlashAttention-2前向吞吐量；4) 关联扫描将递归深度降低至O(log(T/C))，平均吞吐量约为顺序线性注意力的2.4倍；5) 多种KATA变体在长程MQAR和重复键覆盖任务上优于门控DeltaNet；6) 在16倍分布外长度下仍保持0.985 MQAR准确率。

Conclusion: KATA实现了理想的能力-干扰权衡，在常数时间推理下保持高效的关联记忆能力，为特征几何、位置编码、增量规则和衰减门之间的交互提供了深入见解。

Abstract: Linear attention promises constant-time recurrent inference but degrades sharply on associative recall. We formulate attention recall as a spherical-packing problem and introduce Kernelized Linear Attention Activations (KATA), a framework whose feature maps are derived from first principles by certifying nonnegative attention weights through a self-dual homogeneous cone. Building on this observation, we show that rank-one positive semi-definite (PSD) features offer a favorable capacity--interference tradeoff. KATA recovers a parameter-free convex output gate and characterizes associative capacity through the Welch interference floor. For tolerances above this floor, KATA enlarges the state without adding parameters and admits spherical codes with exponentially many keys in the projection dimension. We implement KATA as fused Triton kernels at two operating points: a flash-attention-style forward up to ${\sim}1.6\times$ FlashAttention-2 throughput, and an exact $O(T)$ chunked-state form that reaches ${\sim}11\times$ FlashAttention-2 forward throughput at $131$k tokens. An associative scan of the first-order feature lowers the inter-chunk recurrence depth to $O(\log(T/C))$ for chunk size $C$ and averages ${\sim}2.4\times$ the throughput of a matched sequential linear-attention baseline. On long-range MQAR and repeated-key overwrite, several KATA variants outperform Gated DeltaNet, with parameter counts and state sizes reported alongside accuracy. Induction preserves near-perfect recall, while kernel benchmarks show that the maps can be implemented efficiently. KATA retains $0.985$ MQAR at a $16\times$ out-of-distribution length, approaching the softmax with roughly one quarter of the KV-cache entries. Experiments on 340M-parameter LLMs reveal a feature-dependent fluency trade-off and clarify how positional embeddings, delta rules, and decay gates interact with feature geometry.

</details>


### [128] [Program Synthesis for Simulation-Based Inference: Joint Model Selection and Parameter Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17540)
*Siddharth Mishra-Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 结合大语言模型程序合成与神经模拟推断的联合模型选择与参数估计框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经模拟推断虽能对复杂模型进行参数估计，但通常要求用户指定固定模型结构的模拟器；需要一种方法能够同时进行模型选择（从多种模型家族中识别合适的模型）

Method: 利用大语言模型生成候选模拟器程序，通过反馈驱动的突变迭代优化程序，并采用神经密度估计评估；在模型池上进行模拟推断

Result: 在确定性动力学、随机流行病模型和引力透镜图像暗物质亚结构推断等基准测试上，该方法能从开放提示中识别合理的模型家族，其准确性与数据信息含量和候选模型的可识别性相一致

Conclusion: 该方法实现了对模型池而非仅固定模型内部参数的模拟推断，显著增强了模型选择与参数估计的能力

Abstract: Neural simulation-based inference enables parameter estimation for complex models, but typically requires the user to specify a simulator encoding a fixed model structure. We present a framework for joint model selection and parameter estimation that combines large language models for program synthesis with neural simulation-based inference. Given a natural language description of the system and data under investigation, an LLM proposes candidate simulator programs which are iteratively refined via feedback-driven mutation and evaluated using neural density estimation. The approach enables simulation-based inference over a pool of models, not just parameters within a fixed model. On benchmarks spanning deterministic dynamics, stochastic epidemic models, and dark matter substructure inference from gravitational-lensing images, the method identifies plausible model families from open-ended prompts, with accuracy that reflects the information content of the data and identifiability of candidate models.

</details>


### [129] [AGG: Jacobian-Aggregated Group Gradient for Efficient GRPO Training of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17572)
*Ruiyi Ding,Jie Li,He Kang,Ziyan Liu,Chengru Song,Yuan chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JAGG通过近似中间步骤雅可比矩阵并聚合上游信号，将DiT的RL训练反向传播成本从每步W次降低到每组W步仅2次，在T2I基准上实现约2倍加速且质量损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO算法虽成功用于大语言模型，但扩展到扩散和流匹配模型时存在严重计算瓶颈：需要在采样轨迹的每个时间步对高容量DiT骨干网络进行反向传播，导致高分辨率文本到图像（T2I）训练极其昂贵。

Method: JAGG（Jacobian-Aggregated Group Gradient）方法：1）通过端点雅可比矩阵的t权重插值近似中间步骤雅可比矩阵；2）将每步的上游信号聚合为两个复合梯度；3）通过余弦相似度路由规则（jagg_frac）仅在速度满足线性假设的区域部署JAGG；4）证明了当速度在(z,t)中线性时插值是精确的。

Result: 在T2I基准测试中，JAGG实现了约2倍的反向传播加速，同时保持质量损失可忽略。

Conclusion: DiT隐藏状态和速度预测沿轨迹的线性变化特性可有效用于减少RL训练的反向传播成本，JAGG在保持模型质量的同时显著提高了训练效率。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is a powerful reinforcement learning algorithm for aligning generative models with human preferences. While successful in large language models~\cite{shao2024deepseekmathpushinglimitsmathematical}, its extension to diffusion and flow matching models introduces a severe computational bottleneck: gradients must be back-propagated through the high-capacity DiT backbone at \emph{every} timestep of the sampling trajectory, making high-resolution text-to-image (T2I) training prohibitively expensive. Training-free DiT inference acceleration methods (e.g., $Δ$-DiT, ScalingCache) exploit the fact that DiT hidden states and velocity predictions vary \emph{smoothly and nearly linearly} along the trajectory. We ask whether the same linearity can reduce the backward-pass cost of DiT RL training, and answer affirmatively with \textbf{JAGG} (\textbf{J}acobian-\textbf{A}ggregated \textbf{G}roup \textbf{G}radient), which reduces full transformer backward passes from $W$ to $2$ per group of $W$ consecutive steps. JAGG approximates intermediate-step Jacobians via $t$-weighted interpolation of the endpoint Jacobians, then aggregates per-step upstream signals into two composite gradients applied through a single joint backward pass. We prove this interpolation is \emph{exact} when the velocity is linear in $(z,t)$, and a cosine-similarity routing rule (\texttt{jagg\_frac}) deploys JAGG only where the assumption holds. Experiments on T2I benchmarks show JAGG delivers $\sim$2$\times$ backward speedup with negligible quality degradation.

</details>


### [130] [Planning with Transformers: Chain of Computation and Structured Context Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17710)
*Ehsan Futuhi,Nathan R. Sturtevant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer的迭代式链式计算（COC）架构，通过结构化上下文窗口（SCW）和模式匹配能力，使LLM能够可靠地学习规划策略并解决复杂规划问题。该方法在BlocksWorld和Pancake拼图等任务上达到99.89%以上的成功率，并能解决20个圆盘的汉诺塔问题（需超过100万次动作）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在机器学习各领域影响深远，但近期研究表明其在规划问题求解上存在困难。理论上，现代LLM核心架构Transformer具有图灵完备性，但实际规划性能与理论计算能力之间存在明显差距。本研究旨在探究这一理论计算能力与实证规划表现之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出了链式计算（COC）架构，将基于Transformer的语言模型置于迭代循环中，利用其模式匹配能力。核心组件包括结构化上下文窗口（SCW），提供固定大小的上下文窗口并支持在每一步规划中选择使用哪个窗口。LM在该架构中学习规划策略、预测世界模型并执行规划所需的算术运算。SCW可以是仅追加的（类似图灵机磁带）或使用确定性下推自动机（PDA）形式。

Result: 当使用仅追加的SCW时，即使从零开始训练的小型LM也能学习规划策略，并在每个规划领域的小样本训练实例上实现高泛化能力：在BlocksWorld和Pancake拼图上成功率超过99.89%。对汉诺塔失败案例的分析表明失败主要来自算术运算或遇到未见过的token。COC能够解决最多20个圆盘的汉诺塔问题实例，需要超过100万次动作，且通过符号化算术支持或确定性PDA形式的SCW，训练数据需求显著降低。

Conclusion: COC架构成功弥合了LLM的理论计算能力与实证规划表现之间的鸿沟。通过迭代循环和结构化上下文窗口，Transformer模型能够学习规划策略并执行复杂算术运算，在多个规划域展现出强大的泛化能力。该方法证明了即使小型模型也能通过适当架构设计解决复杂规划问题，为LLM在实际规划任务中的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have had a remarkable impact across many areas of machine learning. However, recent studies have shown that they struggle to reliably solve planning problems. At the same time, theoretical results have shown that transformers, the core architecture underlying modern LLMs, are Turing-complete. In this work, we investigate this apparent gap between the theoretical computational power of LLMs and their empirical planning performance. We propose Chain of Computation (COC), a computational architecture that places a transformer-based LM inside an iterative loop, leveraging its strength as a pattern-matching system. The COC uses a Structured Context Window (SCW) which provides a constant-sized context window with support for choosing which window is used at each planning step. Within this architecture, the LM is able to learn a planning policy, predicts the world model, and performs the arithmetic operations required during planning. We show that, when given an append-only SCW (resembling a Turing Machine tape), even relatively small LMs trained from scratch can learn planning policies and generalize from a small number of training instances within each planning domain, achieving success rates above 99.89\% on BlocksWorld and the Pancake puzzle. Our analysis of failure cases in Tower of Hanoi (TOH) reveals that they arise from arithmetic operations or from encountering previously unseen tokens. We show that COC can solve TOH problem instances with up to 20 disks, requiring over 1 million actions, while requiring substantially less training data by either (1) planning with symbolical support for arithmetic or by (2) using a deterministic pushdown automaton (PDA) formulation for the SCW.

</details>


### [131] [MXSens: Sensitivity-Aware Mixed-Precision Quantization for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17733)
*Simla Burcu Harma,Danila Mishin,Zhengyuan Su,Ayan Chakraborty,Elizaveta Kostenok,Dongho Ha,Babak Falsafi,Martin Jaggi,Yunho Oh,Amir Yazdanbakhsh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MXSens是一种无需训练的混合尾数位宽方法，基于列和层的敏感度自适应分配4/6/8位尾数，在保持资源效率的同时显著提升4-bit量化精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4-bit量化能提高LLM推理效率但受异常值影响导致精度下降。现有方法（数据旋转、混合精度整数量化）常依赖软件管理缩放和频繁反量化，开销较大；微缩放格式（MXINT）虽在硬件中编码缩放且消除了这些开销，但与基于旋转的方法不兼容。

Method: 分析发现异常值严重程度不一且量化敏感度在层和列间分布不均。据此提出MXSens训练-free方法，根据列级和层级敏感度自适应分配混合尾数位宽（4/6/8位），自然利用MXINT的块级结构。

Result: 在多种模型和任务上超越现有量化方法。W4A4KV4设置下，LLaMA-2-70B困惑度为3.77，LLaMA-3-8B为7.63，在WikiText-2上显著优于基线。

Conclusion: 在LLM量化中建立了精度与资源效率的新平衡，无需训练即可实现高效且高精度的4-bit量化。

Abstract: 4-bit quantization enables efficient LLM inference, but suffers from significant accuracy degradation due to outliers. Prior work addresses this problem via data rotation or mixed-precision integer quantization, but often relies on software-managed scaling and frequent dequantization, incurring substantial overhead. Microscaling formats, such as MXINT, eliminate these inefficiencies by encoding scales in hardware, yet remain incompatible with rotation-based methods. Our analysis reveals that outliers vary in severity, from rare extremes to frequent mild deviations, and that quantization sensitivity is unevenly distributed across layers and columns. These insights motivate a fine-grained, sensitivity-guided approach. We introduce MXSens, a training-free method that assigns mixed mantissa bitwidths (4/6/8) based on column- and layer-wise sensitivity, naturally leveraging the block-wise structure of MXINT. MXSens outperforms state-of-the-art quantization methods across a range of models and tasks. Under the W4A4KV4 setting, MXSens achieves perplexities of 3.77 and 7.63 on LLaMA-2-70B and LLaMA-3-8B, respectively, substantially improving over existing baselines on WikiText-2. Our work establishes a new balance between accuracy and resource efficiency for LLM quantization.

</details>


### [132] [FIFA World Cup 2026 as a Contamination-Free Benchmark for LLM Forecasting Agents: Four Models, a Bookmaker, and 104 Matches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17765)
*Jiacheng Ding,Cong Guo,Jason Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了WC2026-Agents基准，评估4个前沿LLM模型作为自主预测代理在2026世界杯104场比赛中的表现，比较其与博彩市场的预测准确率、决策质量和自我认知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大语言模型作为自主预测代理在真实未来事件（2026世界杯）上的能力；基准无污染（比赛在模型训练截止后进行）；需要经济基础基线（博彩市场）来评估预测质量。

Method: 4个前沿模型（Claude Opus 4.8, ChatGPT GPT-5.5, Gemini 3.1 Pro, Grok）在104场世界杯比赛中进行搜索-行动-反思循环：收集证据→预测1X2分布→虚拟下注100美元→比赛后反思。收集赛前博彩市场赔率作为竞争基线。

Result: 4个模型在92%比赛中给出相同首选预测，但均未击败博彩市场；博彩市场Brier分数优于所有模型；朴素平注市场热门胜过所有LLM；模型间投资回报率(-18%至+10%)和错误自报率(36%-86%)差异显著；市场依赖度从12%-100%。

Conclusion: 基准测试衡量模型的校准能力、决策质量和自我认知；即使预测相同，不同模型在决策质量上存在显著差异；模型缺乏市场预测的盈利能力；提供完整数据集和可复现代码。

Abstract: We introduce WC2026-Agents, a benchmark and dataset for evaluating large language models (LLMs) as autonomous forecasting agents on real, future events. For every one of the 104 matches of the 2026 FIFA World Cup, four frontier models -- Claude Opus 4.8, ChatGPT (GPT-5.5, high reasoning), Gemini 3.1 Pro, and Grok (Expert Mode) -- ran an identical search-act-reflect loop: gather evidence with a web tool, commit to a 1X2 (team-A win / draw / team-B win) distribution and a virtual 100-USD bet, and, after the match, reflect given only the final score. Because every match kicked off after the models' training cutoffs, the benchmark is contamination-free by construction. Crucially, we pair the four agents with a fifth competitor drawn from the same information environment -- the pre-match betting market -- collected as per-match 1X2 odds, giving an economically grounded baseline and letting us score not just what an agent predicts but what it does with money. The release contains 416 forecasts and 414 reflections with verbatim reasoning, ground truth (including penalty shootouts), odds, and a reproducible evaluation suite. A reference evaluation surfaces findings that raw accuracy hides: the four agents issue an identical top pick in 92% of matches and none beats the market's Brier score; indeed, a naive flat stake on the market favorite out-earns all four agents. Yet the agents diverge sharply as decision-makers: betting return-on-investment ranges from -18% to +10%, fading the market is unprofitable for all four, the share of forecasts that cite the market ranges from 12% to 100%, and self-reported error rates on wrong picks range from 36% to 86%. The benchmark thus measures calibration, decision quality, and self-knowledge -- axes on which frontier models differ even when their predictions do not. Data and code: https://github.com/graphuofm/FIFA2026LLM

</details>


### [133] [Distributional Soft Bellman Operator under the Cramér Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17897)
*Keru Wang,Yixin Deng,Yao Lyu,Stephen Redmond,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Distributional soft policy iteration (DSPI) combines distributional reinforcement learning with maximum-entropy control. This paper studies the contraction properties of the Cramér geometry metric for the distributional soft Bellman operator in DSPI, proving it's a sqrt(γ)-contraction with a unique fixed point, and shows this follows from a uniform first-moment condition rather than separate boundedness assumptions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Theoretical analysis of distributional soft policy iteration (DSPI) requires understanding whether the distributional soft Bellman operator contracts under a probability metric, with Cramér geometry being a natural choice. The authors want to establish contraction properties to prove existence of unique fixed points and convergence of iterative policy evaluation.

Method: 1. Working directly on an admissible CDF field domain, formulate the CDF-level distributional soft Bellman operator. 2. Prove it's a sqrt(γ)-contraction under the Cramér geometry. 3. Show the finite-Cramér-domain property follows from a uniform first-moment condition on the combined one-step reward entropy shift. 4. Transport the evaluation problem to the spectral domain by conjugation, obtaining an equivalent Hilbert-space representation.

Result: The CDF-level distributional soft Bellman operator is a sqrt(γ)-contraction, there exists a unique fixed point under this metric, the contraction property follows from a uniform first-moment condition rather than separate boundedness assumptions, and a spectral/Hilbert-space representation is obtained.

Conclusion: The Cramér-geometric Bellman fixed point associated with the policy-evaluation step of DSPI is identified, providing a reference point for studying approximate critics, evaluation error, and critic-loss design in DSPI-style algorithms.

Abstract: Distributional soft policy iteration (DSPI) provides an important framework for combining distributional reinforcement learning (DRL) with maximum-entropy control, in which the policy evaluation step is governed by a distributional soft Bellman operator acting on entropy-regularised returns. Theoretical analysis of such an evaluation step requires a probability metric under which Bellman updates can be controlled, typically by showing that the operator contracts the distance between any two candidate return-distribution estimates. In this paper, we focus on the Cramér geometry, a cumulative distribution function (CDF)-based metric with an $L^2$ structure, and study whether the fixed-policy distributional soft Bellman operator has this contraction property and hence a unique fixed point under this metric. Working directly on an admissible CDF field domain, we formulate the CDF-level distributional soft Bellman operator, prove that it is a $\sqrtγ$-contraction, and obtain the corresponding unique fixed point together with convergent iterative policy evaluation. The CDF formulation also shows that this finite-Cramér-domain property follows from a uniform first-moment condition on the combined one-step reward entropy shift, rather than from separate uniform boundedness assumptions on the reward and entropy terms. We then transport the same evaluation problem to the spectral domain by conjugation, obtaining an equivalent Hilbert-space representation of the same decision process. Taken together, these results identify the Cramér-geometric Bellman fixed point associated with the policy-evaluation step of DSPI, providing a reference point for studying approximate critics, evaluation error, and critic-loss design in DSPI-style algorithms.

</details>


### [134] [A Geometric Perspective on Stabilizing Value Conflict Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17946)
*Saket Reddy,Andy Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM在RLHF中面临价值冲突优化不稳定问题，本文研究表明链式思维(CoT)通过平滑损失景观的最陡方向来解决这一挑战，并提出新的价值冲突导向CoT设计，提升了道德推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在基于强化学习人类反馈(RLHF)的训练中，常因使用压缩的标量奖励而难以处理价值冲突，导致优化不稳定。

Method: 通过几何分析展示CoT能够平滑损失景观的最陡方向，并创建了一种新的价值冲突导向CoT设计，进一步优化推理动态。

Result: CoT通过平滑损失景观帮助解决了传统标量奖励的优化不稳定问题；在下游基准测试中，价值冲突导向的CoT能够泛化到各种道德推理任务；新的CoT设计进一步平滑损失景观并提升了道德推理性能。

Conclusion: 明确修改和改进推理动态的设计，为提升LLM处理复杂价值冲突请求的性能提供了有效途径，推动了LLM的多元对齐。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often struggle to navigate value conflicts when trained with the compressed scalar rewards of Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF). To address this challenge, we investigate how chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning can help improve performance in this domain. Geometrically, we show that CoT correlates with further smoothing the model's loss landscape in its sharpest direction, helping resolve the optimization instability of traditional scalar rewards. We also demonstrate via relevant downstream benchmarks that value conflict-focused CoT may generalize to different kinds of moral reasoning, demonstrating that this CoT has the potential to be an effective mechanism for better moral reasoning. To capitalize on this potential, we create a new value conflict-focused CoT design that further smooths the sharpest direction of the loss landscape and increases moral reasoning performance. This finding shows that explicitly modifying and improving the design of reasoning dynamics offers a promising avenue for improving model performance on user requests with complex value conflicts, advancing pluralistic alignment in LLMs.

</details>


### [135] [SelectInfer: Selective Neuron Loading and Computation for On-Device LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18081)
*Huzaifa Shaaban Kabakibo,Eric Schniedermeyer,Artem Burchanow,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SelectInfer是一个神经元级别的优化框架，通过选择性神经元加载和计算来减少LLM的内存占用和计算量，同时保持模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在边缘设备部署面临高计算和内存需求的挑战；现有方法（粗粒度剪枝/量化）可能损害精度或需要重新训练。

Method: 1) 使用离线LLM分析器进行神经元特征分析；2) 识别任务特定和通用神经元；3) 选择性加载：只加载最重要的神经元子集；4) 选择性计算：动态计算最相关的神经元。

Result: 在多个数据集上验证，SelectInfer显著减少了内存占用和计算量，同时保持了任务性能。

Conclusion: SelectInfer为在边缘设备上部署LLM提供了一种实用且有效的解决方案，平衡了性能与资源约束。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across a range of Natural Language Processing (NLP) tasks, but their high computational and memory demands pose significant challenges for deployment on resource-constrained edge devices. Existing approaches to model compression and optimization often rely on coarse-grained pruning or quantization, which can compromise accuracy or require re-training and fine-tuning. In this work, we introduce SelectInfer, a neuron-level optimization framework that enables efficient LLM inference on edge devices through selective neuron loading and computation. By profiling and identifying both task-specific and general-purpose neurons using an offline LLM profiler, SelectInfer implements two key optimizations: selective loading, which reduces memory footprint by selectively loading a subset of neurons that were identified to be most important during the offline stage, and selective computation, which dynamically computes only the most relevant neurons at runtime. Evaluation across multiple datasets shows that SelectInfer achieves significant reductions in memory footprint and computation while preserving task performance, making it a practical step towards enabling LLM deployment on edge devices

</details>


### [136] [Enhancing Rubric-based RL via Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18082)
*Mingxuan Xia,Yuhang Yang,Chao Ye,Shuai Zhu,Shenzhi Yang,Guangcheng Zhu,Yuhang Zhang,Cheng Peng,Haobo Wang,Siqing Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Rubric-based RL在LLM开放任务中存在探索受限问题，论文提出CriPO方法同时解决未探索标准（UC）和被抑制标准（SC）两种失败模式，通过on-policy自蒸馏增强策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rubric-based RL的主要限制是探索受限，具体表现为两种失败模式：1）未探索标准（UC）- 没有任何rollout满足的标准；2）被抑制标准（SC）- 虽然被满足但学习信号被丢失的标准。现有方法要么仅关注UC，要么引入训练-推理不匹配，导致错误累积。

Method: CriPO（Criterion-Distilled Policy Optimization）采用on-policy自蒸馏方法：1）对UC，构建criterion-injection self-teacher并计算localized forward-KL loss注入缺失行为；2）对SC，使用counterfactual self-teacher定位负优势rollout中的相关token并翻转其token-level优势为正值。

Result: 在医学和科学基准测试中，CriPO consistently outperforms rubric-based RL，在约2倍更少的优化步骤中达到更强的最终性能。分析显示57%的样本在整个训练过程中表现出SC失败模式，平均每样本1.8个SC。

Conclusion: CriPO同时解决了UC和SC问题，无需引入训练-推理不匹配，通过自蒸馏机制有效提升LLM在rubric-based RL中的探索能力和学习效率。

Abstract: Rubric-based RL has recently shown promise in improving LLMs on open-ended tasks. A widely recognized limitation of rubric-based RL is limited exploration: criteria that no rollout manages to satisfy (Unexplored Criteria, UC) receive no optimization signal. Recent methods address this by incorporating rubric information as external guidance during rollout, yet they introduce a train-inference mismatch: the policy is optimized on rollouts produced under external guidance while this guidance is absent at inference time, causing error accumulation through autoregressive decoding. Moreover, these exploration-focused approaches overlook a fundamentally different failure mode that we term Suppressed Criteria (SC) -- criteria that are satisfied by some rollouts yet whose learning signals are lost during optimization because scalar reward aggregation assigns them non-positive aggregate advantages. Our analysis reveals that SC are remarkably prevalent: over 57% of samples exhibit this failure mode throughout training, with an average of 1.8 SC per sample. To simultaneously address both UC and SC without introducing training-inference mismatch, we propose Criterion-Distilled Policy Optimization (CriPO), which enhances rubric-based RL via on-policy self-distillation. For UC, CriPO constructs a criterion-injection self-teacher and computes a localized forward-KL loss to inject missing behaviors into the policy. For SC, CriPO employs a counterfactual self-teacher to locate criterion-relevant tokens in negative-advantage rollouts and flips their token-level advantages to positive values, preserving useful patterns that would otherwise be suppressed. Experiments on medicine and science benchmarks demonstrate that CriPO consistently outperforms rubric-based RL, achieving stronger final performance with approximately $2\times$ fewer optimization steps.

</details>


### [137] [OR Else: A Differentiable Trust Region for Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18163)
*Chinmay Rane,Kanishka Tyagi,Michael Manry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究对比了强化学习优化方法PPO和GRPO在LLM后训练中的表现，比较了传统的裁剪目标函数与新的平滑单侧饱和规则Output Reset (OR)的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PPO和GRPO使用的裁剪代理目标函数在有利方向饱和时会导致标量目标函数的导数出现突变，作者探究平滑的单侧饱和规则OR是否能为LLM后训练提供更优的替代方案。

Method: 在Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct模型上使用Anthropic的hh-rlhf数据集进行实验，比较了PPO-clip与PPO-OR（基于GAE）、GRPO与GRPO-OR（基于组相对优势）四种配置，每组方法使用三个随机种子，评估指标为训练时的奖励模型分数。

Result: 在GAE设置下，PPO-OR比PPO-clip的平均最终奖励高0.305；在组相对优势设置下，GRPO-OR展现出更小的分数波动、接近零的终端OR残差和下降的超调比例，但在G=2时未显示出奖励分数提升；两种方法都表现出比GAE方法更大的rollout-to-current对数比率位移。

Conclusion: OR在两种匹配比较中都改变了优化行为，但观察到的奖励效应有所不同：在GAE下有正向收益，在组相对优势下仅改善稳定性指标。GRPO-OR在G=2时未带来奖励分数提升，更大组数的影响尚不明确。需要注意的是，报告分数是训练时的奖励模型测量值，而非离线的人类偏好性能。

Abstract: PPO and the GRPO baseline studied here use clipped surrogate objectives whose favorable-direction saturation introduces an abrupt change in the scalar objective's derivative. We ask whether Output Reset (OR), a smooth one-sided saturation rule, offers a useful alternative for large language model post-training. PPO-OR and GRPO-OR replace the clipped policy term with an OR squared-margin loss in rollout-relative token log-ratio space; the advantage sign determines the update direction, and a token contributes zero direct OR residual after crossing the favorable margin. We compare PPO-clip with PPO-OR under generalized advantage estimation (GAE), and GRPO with GRPO-OR under group-relative advantages, using \texttt{Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct} on Anthropic \texttt{hh-rlhf} with one shared reward model and three seeds per method. Under GAE, PPO-OR has a mean final training-time reward-model score $0.305$ higher than PPO-clip, with a larger observed across-seed spread. Under group-relative advantages, GRPO-OR does not have a higher mean score, but shows a smaller observed spread, a near-zero terminal OR residual, and a declining overshoot fraction, while the matched GRPO clipped-objective trace remains variable. Both group-relative methods exhibit substantially larger rollout-to-current log-ratio displacement than the GAE methods, and OR does not consistently reduce it. Thus, OR changes optimization behavior in both matched comparisons, but the observed reward effect differs between them. At $G=2$, the GRPO-OR diagnostics do not translate into a reward-score gain. Whether larger groups change this outcome remains open. The reported scores are training-time reward-model measurements, not held-out human-preference performance.

</details>


### [138] [FlashRT: Agent Harness for Guiding Agents to Deploy Real-Time Multimodal Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18171)
*Krish Agarwal,Zhuoming Chen,Yanyuan Qin,Zhenyu Gu,Atri Rudra,Beidi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlashRT是一种智能体框架，通过链式编程范式将简单的参考实现自动转化为优化的多GPU部署方案，在各类实时多模态应用中实现高达70倍延迟降低和2.8-3.6倍吞吐量提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时多模态应用需要针对部署进行应用特定的放置、流式传输和模型内并行化决策。现有服务系统和自动并行编译器受限于转换方式和固定工作负载假设，导致实现高性能需要人工手工优化

Method: FlashRT采用链式编程范式，通过多阶段转换过程：1) 参考实现→中间表示(IR)以捕获数据依赖和持久状态作用域；2) 通过顺序解释器验证IR；3) 静态分析识别候选转换；4) 在测量门控优化循环中迭代实现、验证和基准测试每个候选

Result: 在NVIDIA B200 GPU上实现高达~70倍延迟降低和2.8倍吞吐量提升；在AMD MI355X GPU上匹配峰值延迟降低并实现3.6倍吞吐量提升；在Qwen3-Omni文本转音频推理中比专家实现的vLLM-Omni减少65%响应延迟

Conclusion: 智能体驱动的优化在优化成熟度较低的平台上也具有可扩展性，能够为NVIDIA和AMD硬件提供更优性能，证明智能体优化方法的有效性和普适性

Abstract: Real-time multimodal applications, including voice agents and interactive video generation, compose heterogeneous models into pipelines whose efficient deployment requires application-specific decisions about placement, streaming, and intra-model parallelism. Existing serving systems and auto-parallelism compilers commit to limited transformations and fixed workload assumptions, so achieving high performance on a new application requires hand-crafting an efficient implementation. We present FlashRT, an agent harness that guides coding agents to lift simple developer-written reference implementations into optimized multi-GPU deployments that flexibly weigh target metrics like latency and throughput. Using a new chain-of-program paradigm, FlashRT directs a generic coding agent through a multi-pass transformation process where an agent transforms the reference into an intermediate representation (IR) to capture data dependencies and persistent-state scopes, validates this IR via a sequential interpreter, and performs static analyses to identify candidate transformations. Then, the agent iteratively implements, verifies, and benchmarks each candidate under a measurement-gated optimization loop to produce effective deployments that span different hardware budgets. Across various applications, including video world models and multimodal LLMs, FlashRT converts reference implementations into highly efficient deployments, delivering up to ~70x latency reduction and 2.8x throughput improvement on NVIDIA B200 GPUs. On AMD MI355X GPUs, FlashRT matches the peak latency reduction while increasing peak throughput improvement to 3.6x, demonstrating that agent-driven optimization can be more scalable on platforms with less mature expert optimization. In fact, for Qwen3-Omni text-to-audio inference, FlashRT reduces response latency by 65% compared to the expert vLLM-Omni implementation on AMD MI355X.

</details>
